Handling Missing Values\n\n```python\n# Drop rows with any missing values\ndf_clean = df.dropna()\n\n# Drop rows with missing values in specific columns\ndf_clean = df.dropna(subset=['column1', 'column2'])\n\n# Check for missing values\nprint(df.isnull().sum())\n```\n\n### 3. Clinical Lab Unit Conversions for CKD Data\n\n## Overview\nThis skill document provides reference conversion factors for common clinical laboratory tests, focusing on converting between SI units and US conventional units used in CKD (Chronic Kidney Disease) monitoring.\n\n## Kidney Function Markers\n\n### Serum Creatinine\n- **US Conventional Unit**: mg/dL\n- **SI Unit**: µmol/L\n- **Conversion**: µmol/L ÷ 88.4 = mg/dL\n- **Normal Range (US)**: 0.6-1.2 mg/dL (varies by age/sex)\n- **CKD Range**: May be elevated up to 15+ mg/dL in severe CKD\n\n### Blood Urea Nitrogen (BUN)\n- **US Conventional Unit**: mg/dL\n- **SI Unit**: mmol/L\n- **Conversion**: mmol/L × 2.8 = mg/dL\n- **Normal Range (US)**: 7-20 mg/dL\n- **CKD Range**: May be elevated up to 100+ mg/dL\n\n### eGFR\n- **Standard Unit**: mL/min/1.73m²\n- **Normal Range**: 90-120 mL/min/1.73m²\n- **CKD Staging**: Stage 1: ≥90, Stage 2: 60-89, Stage 3: 30-59, Stage 4: 15-29, Stage 5: <15\n\n### Cystatin C\n- **US Conventional Unit**: mg/L\n- **Normal Range**: 0.5-1.0 mg/L\n- **CKD Range**: May be elevated up to 10+ mg/L\n\n## Electrolytes\n\n### Sodium\n- **Unit**: mEq/L or mmol/L (same value)\n- **Normal Range**: 136-145 mEq/L\n\n### Potassium\n- **Unit**: mEq/L or mmol/L (same value)\n- **Normal Range**: 3.5-5.0 mEq/L\n- **CKD consideration**: Often elevated in CKD (up to 7+ in severe cases)\n\n### Chloride\n- **Unit**: mEq/L or mmol/L (same value)\n- **Normal Range**: 98-106 mEq/L\n\n### Bicarbonate\n- **Unit**: mEq/L or mmol/L (same value)\n- **Normal Range**: 22-29 mEq/L\n\n### Magnesium\n- **US Conventional Unit**: mg/dL\n- **SI Unit**: mmol/L\n- **Conversion**: mmol/L × 2.43 = mg/dL\n- **Normal Range (US)**: 1.7-2.4 mg/dL\n\n## Calcium and Bone Metabolism\n\n### Serum Calcium (Total)\n- **US Conventional Unit**: mg/dL\n- **SI Unit**: mmol/L\n- **Conversion**: mmol/L × 4.0 = mg/dL\n- **Normal Range (US)**: 8.5-10.5 mg/dL\n\n### Ionized Calcium\n- **US Conventional Unit**: mg/dL or mmol/L\n- **Conversion**: mmol/L × 4.0 = mg/dL\n- **Normal Range (US)**: 4.5-5.3 mg/dL or 1.12-1.32 mmol/L\n\n### Phosphorus\n- **US Conventional Unit**: mg/dL\n- **SI Unit**: mmol/L\n- **Conversion**: mmol/L × 3.1 = mg/dL\n- **Normal Range (US)**: 2.5-4.5 mg/dL\n- **CKD Range**: Often elevated up to 10+ mg/dL\n\n### Intact PTH\n- **US Conventional Unit**: pg/mL\n- **SI Unit**: ng/L (same value)\n- **Normal Range**: 15-65 pg/mL\n- **CKD Range**: Often elevated up to 2000+ pg/mL in secondary hyperparathyroidism\n\n### 25-Hydroxyvitamin D\n- **US Conventional Unit**: ng/mL\n- **SI Unit**: nmol/L\n- **Conversion**: nmol/L ÷ 2.496 = ng/mL\n- **Normal Range (US)**: 30-100 ng/mL\n\n### 1,25-Dihydroxyvitamin D\n- **US Conventional Unit**: pg/mL\n- **SI Unit**: pmol/L\n- **Conversion**: pmol/L ÷ 2.6 = pg/mL\n- **Normal Range (US)**: 15-60 pg/mL\n\n### Alkaline Phosphatase\n- **Unit**: U/L or IU/L (same)\n- **Normal Range**: 44-147 U/L\n\n## Hematology\n\n### Hemoglobin\n- **US Conventional Unit**: g/dL\n- **SI Unit**: g/L\n- **Conversion**: g/L ÷ 10 = g/dL\n- **Normal Range (US)**: 12-17 g/dL (varies by sex)\n- **CKD Range**: Often low, 7-12 g/dL\n\n### Hematocrit\n- **Unit**: % (fraction × 100)\n- **Normal Range**: 36-50%\n\n### RBC Count\n- **Unit**: million/µL or 10^12/L\n- **Normal Range**: 4.0-6.0 million/µL\n\n### WBC Count\n- **US Conventional Unit**: thousand/µL or K/µL\n- **SI Unit**: 10^9/L (same value)\n- **Normal Range**: 4.0-11.0 K/µL\n\n### Platelet Count\n- **US Conventional Unit**: thousand/µL or K/µL\n- **SI Unit**: 10^9/L (same value)\n- **Normal Range**: 150-400 K/µL\n\n## Iron Studies\n\n### Serum Iron\n- **US Conventional Unit**: µg/dL\n- **SI Unit**: µmol/L\n- **Conversion**: µmol/L × 5.587 = µg/dL\n- **Normal Range (US)**: 60-170 µg/dL\n\n### TIBC\n- **US Conventional Unit**: µg/dL\n- **SI Unit**: µmol/L\n- **Conversion**: µmol/L × 5.587 = µg/dL\n- **Normal Range (US)**: 250-400 µg/dL\n\n### Transferrin Saturation\n- **Unit**: %\n- **Normal Range**: 20-50%\n\n### Ferritin\n- **Unit**: ng/mL or µg/L (same value)\n- **Normal Range**: 12-300 ng/mL\n- **CKD Target**: Often 200-500 ng/mL for ESA therapy\n\n### Reticulocyte Count\n- **Unit**: %\n- **Normal Range**: 0.5-2.5%\n\n## Liver Function and Bilirubin\n\n### Total Bilirubin\n- **US Conventional Unit**: mg/dL\n- **SI Unit**: µmol/L\n- **Conversion**: µmol/L ÷ 17.1 = mg/dL\n- **Normal Range (US)**: 0.1-1.2 mg/dL\n\n### Direct Bilirubin\n- **US Conventional Unit**: mg/dL\n- **SI Unit**: µmol/L\n- **Conversion**: µmol/L ÷ 17.1 = mg/dL\n- **Normal Range (US)**: 0.0-0.3 mg/dL\n\n### Albumin (Serum)\n- **US Conventional Unit**: g/dL\n- **SI Unit**: g/L\n- **Conversion**: g/L ÷ 10 = g/dL\n- **Normal Range (US)**: 3.5-5.0 {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_vrtx_01Q84hvdsd6pNEchiDovV8wN","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_015vNfvy3zUAeVhDeV3VvG6w","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/unit-harmonization-algorithm.md","content":"# Unit Harmonization Algorithm for Clinical Lab Data\n\n## Overview\nThis skill document describes the algorithm for detecting and correcting unit inconsistencies in clinical laboratory data, particularly for CKD (Chronic Kidney Disease) datasets.\n\n## Problem Statement\nClinical lab data from multiple healthcare systems often uses different unit systems:\n- SI Units (International System) - common in Europe and scientific literature\n- US Conventional Units - common in US healthcare\n\nValues outside physiological ranges often indicate wrong units rather than true outliers.\n\n## Detection Strategy\n\n### Step 1: Define Expected Ranges\nFor each analyte, define:\n1. **Primary Range**: Physiologically plausible values in US conventional units\n2. **Alternative Unit Range**: Expected range if values were in SI units\n3. **Conversion Factor**: To convert from SI to US conventional\n\n### Step 2: Classification Logic\n\n```python\ndef classify_value(value, primary_min, primary_max, conversion_factor):\n    \"\"\"\n    Classify whether a value needs unit conversion.\n\n    Returns:\n        'normal' - value is within expected range\n        'convert' - value appears to be in alternative units\n        'outlier' - true physiological outlier (rare)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Check if in primary (US) range\n    if primary_min <= value <= primary_max:\n        return 'normal'\n\n    # Check if converting would put it in range\n    converted = value / conversion_factor  # or * depending on direction\n    if primary_min <= converted <= primary_max:\n        return 'convert'\n\n    # Value is a true outlier\n    return 'outlier'\n```\n\n## Conversion Reference Table\n\n| Analyte | US Unit | SI Unit | Conversion (SI→US) | US Range |\n|---------|---------|---------|-------------------|----------|\n| Serum Creatinine | mg/dL | µmol/L | ÷ 88.4 | 0.5-15 |\n| BUN | mg/dL | mmol/L | × 2.8 | 5-200 |\n| Hemoglobin | g/dL | g/L | ÷ 10 | 5-20 |\n| Glucose | mg/dL | mmol/L | × 18.02 | 40-800 |\n| Calcium | mg/dL | mmol/L | × 4.0 | 6-14 |\n| Cholesterol | mg/dL | mmol/L | × 38.67 | 50-400 |\n\n## Implementation Pattern\n\n```python\ndef harmonize_column(df, column, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single column based on configuration.\n\n    config = {\n        'us_min': float,      # Expected minimum in US units\n        'us_max': float,      # Expected maximum in US units\n        'si_to_us': float,    # Multiplication factor to convert SI→US\n        'direction': str      # 'multiply' or 'divide'\n    }\n    \"\"\"\n    def convert_value(val):\n        if pd.isna(val):\n            return val\n\n        # Already in range\n        if config['us_min'] <= val <= config['us_max']:\n            return val\n\n        # Try conversion\n        if config['direction'] == 'divide':\n            converted = val / config['si_to_us']\n        else:\n            converted = val * config['si_to_us']\n\n        # Check if conversion helps\n        if config['us_min'] <= converted <= config['us_max']:\n            return converted\n\n        # Keep original if conversion doesn't help\n        return val\n\n    return df[column].apply(convert_value)\n```\n\n## Edge Cases\n\n### 1. Overlapping Ranges\nSome analytes have overlapping ranges between units. Use broader context:\n- Compare with related analytes (e.g., if hemoglobin is in g/L, hematocrit might also need conversion)\n- Use population-level statistics\n\n### 2. Extreme CKD Values\nCKD patients can have extreme values that appear out of range:\n- Set wider ranges for CKD-specific markers\n- Document the expected ranges for CKD population\n\n### 3. Multiple Possible Units\nSome tests can use multiple units:\n- Prioritize the most common alternative unit\n- Log ambiguous cases for manual review\n\n## Validation Steps\n\nAfter harmonization:\n1. Check all values are within expected physiological ranges\n2. Verify no impossible values (negative concentrations, etc.)\n3. Compare distributions before/after conversion\n4. Spot-check a sample of converted values\n\n## Example Implementation\n\n```python\n# Configuration for common CKD lab values\nHARMONIZATION_CONFIG = {\n    'Serum_Creatinine': {\n        'us_min': 0.3, 'us_max': 20,\n        'si_to_us': 88.4, 'direction': 'divide'\n    },\n    'Hemoglobin': {\n        'us_min': 5, 'us_max': 22,\n        'si_to_us': 10, 'direction': 'divide'\n    },\n    'Glucose': {\n        'us_min': 30, 'us_max': 1000,\n        'si_to_us': 18.02, 'direction': 'multiply'\n    }\n}\n\nfor column, config in HARMONIZATION_CONFIG.items():\n    df[column] = harmonize_column(df, column, config)\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Document all conversions**: Keep a log of which values were converted\n2. **Preserve original data**: Always work on a copy\n3. **Use domain knowledge**: Clinical ranges may vary by patient population\n4. **Validate with domain experts**: Have clinicians review edge cases\n5. **Test with known data**: Use test cases with known units to validate logic\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":227,"cache_read_input_tokens":28339,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":227,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":7},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b062c6fe-fde0-43a5-a396-cb627c6eec56","uuid":"9b2072f4-baf3-4c7e-bd12-1e834437da9c"}

Loading Data with Mixed Formats\n\nWhen loading CSV files with European-style decimals (commas instead of periods):\n\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Load data\ndf = pd.read_csv('data.csv')\n\n# Convert European decimal format (comma to period)\ndef convert_european_decimal(value):\n    if isinstance(value, str):\n        # Replace comma with period for decimal\n        return value.replace(',', '.')\n    return value\n```\n\n### 2. Handling Missing Values\n\n```python\n# Drop rows with any missing values\ndf_clean = df.dropna()\n\n# Drop rows with missing values in specific columns\ndf_clean = df.dropna(subset=['column1', 'column2'])\n\n# Check for missing values\nprint(df.isnull().sum())\n```\n\n### 3. Scientific Notation Handling\n\n```python\n# Convert scientific notation strings to float\ndef parse_numeric(value):\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):\n        return float(value)\n    if isinstance(value, str):\n        # Replace comma decimal separator\n        value = value.replace(',', '.')\n        try:\n            return float(value)  # Handles scientific notation automatically\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n    return value\n\n# Apply to dataframe\nfor col in numeric_columns:\n    df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric)\n```\n\n### 4. Rounding Values\n\n```python\n# Round to 2 decimal places\ndf[numeric_columns] = df[numeric_columns].round(2)\n\n# Format as string with exactly 2 decimal places\ndf[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\")\n```\n\n### 5. Data Validation\n\n```python\n# Check value ranges\ndef check_range(df, column, min_val, max_val):\n    mask = (df[column] >= min_val) & (df[column] <= max_val)\n    outliers = df[~mask]\n    return outliers\n\n# Identify outliers that may need unit conversion\noutliers = check_range(df, 'Hemoglobin', 5, 20)  # g/dL range\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. Always make a copy of original data before modifications\n2. Log transformations applied for reproducibility\n3. Validate data ranges after cleaning\n4. Clinical Lab Unit Conversions for CKD Data\n\n## Overview\nThis skill document provides reference conversion factors for common clinical laboratory tests, focusing on converting between SI units and US conventional units used in CKD (Chronic Kidney Disease) monitoring.\n\n## Kidney Function Markers\n\n### Serum Creatinine\n- **US Conventional Unit**: mg/dL\n- **SI Unit**: µmol/L\n- **Conversion**: µmol/L ÷ 88.4 = mg/dL\n- **Normal Range (US)**: 0.6-1.2 mg/dL (varies by age/sex)\n- **CKD Range**: May be elevated up to 15+ mg/dL in severe CKD\n\n### Blood Urea Nitrogen (BUN)\n- **US Conventional Unit**: mg/dL\n- **SI Unit**: mmol/L\n- **Conversion**: mmol/L × 2.8 = mg/dL\n- **Normal Range (US)**: 7-20 mg/dL\n- **CKD Range**: May be elevated up to 100+ mg/dL\n\n### eGFR\n- **Standard Unit**: mL/min/1.73m²\n- **Normal Range**: 90-120 mL/min/1.73m²\n- **CKD Staging**: Stage 1: ≥90, Stage 2: 60-89, Stage 3: 30-59, Stage 4: 15-29, Stage 5: <15\n\n### Cystatin C\n- **US Conventional Unit**: mg/L\n- **Normal Range**: 0.5-1.0 mg/L\n- **CKD Range**: May be elevated up to 10+ mg/L\n\n## Electrolytes\n\n### Sodium\n- **Unit**: mEq/L or mmol/L (same value)\n- **Normal Range**: 136-145 mEq/L\n\n### Potassium\n- **Unit**: mEq/L or mmol/L (same value)\n- **Normal Range**: 3.5-5.0 mEq/L\n- **CKD consideration**: Often elevated in CKD (up to 7+ in severe cases)\n\n### Chloride\n- **Unit**: mEq/L or mmol/L (same value)\n- **Normal Range**: 98-106 mEq/L\n\n### Bicarbonate\n- **Unit**: mEq/L or mmol/L (same value)\n- **Normal Range**: 22-29 mEq/L\n\n### Magnesium\n- **US Conventional Unit**: mg/dL\n- **SI Unit**: mmol/L\n- **Conversion**: mmol/L × 2.43 = mg/dL\n- **Normal Range (US)**: 1.7-2.4 mg/dL\n\n## Calcium and Bone Metabolism\n\n### Serum Calcium {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_vrtx_015vNfvy3zUAeVhDeV3VvG6w","type":"tool_result","content":"File created successfully at: /root/environment/skills/unit-harmonization-algorithm.md"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b062c6fe-fde0-43a5-a396-cb627c6eec56","uuid":"922f7715-9901-4c3d-a936-b1cbe9b1fbb9","tool_use_result":{"type":"create","filePath":"/root/environment/skills/unit-harmonization-algorithm.md","content":"# Unit Harmonization Algorithm for Clinical Lab Data\n\n## Overview\nThis skill document describes the algorithm for detecting and correcting unit inconsistencies in clinical laboratory data, particularly for CKD (Chronic Kidney Disease) datasets.\n\n## Problem Statement\nClinical lab data from multiple healthcare systems often uses different unit systems:\n- SI Units (International System) - common in Europe and scientific literature\n- US Conventional Units - common in US healthcare\n\nValues outside physiological ranges often indicate wrong units rather than true outliers.\n\n## Detection Strategy\n\n### Step 1: Define Expected Ranges\nFor each analyte, define:\n1. **Primary Range**: Physiologically plausible values in US conventional units\n2. **Alternative Unit Range**: Expected range if values were in SI units\n3. **Conversion Factor**: To convert from SI to US conventional\n\n### Step 2: Classification Logic\n\n```python\ndef classify_value(value, primary_min, primary_max, conversion_factor):\n    \"\"\"\n    Classify whether a value needs unit conversion.\n\n    Returns:\n        'normal' - value is within expected range\n        'convert' - value appears to be in alternative units\n        'outlier' - true physiological outlier (rare)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Check if in primary (US) range\n    if primary_min <= value <= primary_max:\n        return 'normal'\n\n    # Check if converting would put it in range\n    converted = value / conversion_factor  # or * depending on direction\n    if primary_min <= converted <= primary_max:\n        return 'convert'\n\n    # Value is a true outlier\n    return 'outlier'\n```\n\n## Conversion Reference Table\n\n| Analyte | US Unit | SI Unit | Conversion (SI→US) | US Range |\n|---------|---------|---------|-------------------|----------|\n| Serum Creatinine | mg/dL | µmol/L | ÷ 88.4 | 0.5-15 |\n| BUN | mg/dL | mmol/L | × 2.8 | 5-200 |\n| Hemoglobin | g/dL | g/L | ÷ 10 | 5-20 |\n| Glucose | mg/dL | mmol/L | × 18.02 | 40-800 |\n| Calcium | mg/dL | mmol/L | × 4.0 | 6-14 |\n| Cholesterol | mg/dL | mmol/L | × 38.67 | 50-400 |\n\n## Implementation Pattern\n\n```python\ndef harmonize_column(df, column, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single column based on configuration.\n\n    config = {\n        'us_min': float,      # Expected minimum in US units\n        'us_max': float,      # Expected maximum in US units\n        'si_to_us': float,    # Multiplication factor to convert SI→US\n        'direction': str      # 'multiply' or 'divide'\n    }\n    \"\"\"\n    def convert_value(val):\n        if pd.isna(val):\n            return val\n\n        # Already in range\n        if config['us_min'] <= val <= config['us_max']:\n            return val\n\n        # Try conversion\n        if config['direction'] == 'divide':\n            converted = val / config['si_to_us']\n        else:\n            converted = val * config['si_to_us']\n\n        # Check if conversion helps\n        if config['us_min'] <= converted <= config['us_max']:\n            return converted\n\n        # Keep original if conversion doesn't help\n        return val\n\n    return df[column].apply(convert_value)\n```\n\n## Edge Cases\n\n### 1. Overlapping Ranges\nSome analytes have overlapping ranges between units. Use broader context:\n- Compare with related analytes (e.g., if hemoglobin is in g/L, hematocrit might also need conversion)\n- Use population-level statistics\n\n### 2. Extreme CKD Values\nCKD patients can have extreme values that appear out of range:\n- Set wider ranges for CKD-specific markers\n- Document the expected ranges for CKD population\n\n### 3. Multiple Possible Units\nSome tests can use multiple units:\n- Prioritize the most common alternative unit\n- Log ambiguous cases for manual review\n\n## Validation Steps\n\nAfter harmonization:\n1. Check all values are within expected physiological ranges\n2. Verify no impossible values (negative concentrations, etc.)\n3. Compare distributions before/after conversion\n4. Spot-check a sample of converted values\n\n## Example Implementation\n\n```python\n# Configuration for common CKD lab values\nHARMONIZATION_CONFIG = {\n    'Serum_Creatinine': {\n        'us_min': 0.3, 'us_max': 20,\n        'si_to_us': 88.4, 'direction': 'divide'\n    },\n    'Hemoglobin': {\n        'us_min': 5, 'us_max': 22,\n        'si_to_us': 10, 'direction': 'divide'\n    },\n    'Glucose': {\n        'us_min': 30, 'us_max': 1000,\n        'si_to_us': 18.02, 'direction': 'multiply'\n    }\n}\n\nfor column, config in HARMONIZATION_CONFIG.items():\n    df[column] = harmonize_column(df, column, config)\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Document all conversions**: Keep a log of which values were converted\n2. **Preserve original data**: Always work on a copy\n3. **Use domain knowledge**: Clinical ranges may vary by patient population\n4. **Validate with domain experts**: Have clinicians review edge cases\n5. **Test with known data**: Use test cases with known units to validate logic\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

µg/L\n        'conversion_factor': 1, 'direction': 'multiply'\n    },\n}\n\n\ndef parse_numeric_value(value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Parse a numeric value, handling:\n    - Scientific notation (e.g., 1.23e+02)\n    - European decimal format (comma as decimal separator)\n    - Missing/empty values\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):\n        return float(value)\n\n    if isinstance(value, str):\n        # Remove any whitespace\n        value = value.strip()\n        if value == '' or value.lower() == 'nan':\n            return np.nan\n\n        # Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n        # Be careful: only replace comma if it's a decimal separator\n        # European format: \"12,34\" means 12.34\n        # Check if there's already a period\n        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n        try:\n            # Python's float() handles scientific notation automatically\n            return float(value)\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n\n    return np.nan\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n    If value is outside expected range, try converting from alternative units.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    factor = config['conversion_factor']\n    direction = config['direction']\n\n    # Check if already in expected US range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # Try conversion from alternative units\n    if direction == 'divide':\n        converted = value / factor\n    else:  # multiply\n        converted = value * factor\n\n    # Check if converted value is in range\n    if us_min <= converted <= us_max:\n        return converted\n\n    # If conversion doesn't help, keep original value\n    # (might be a true outlier or need manual review)\n    return value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"\n    Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\n    Returns string in format X.XX\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    # Read the input data\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    # Get numeric columns (all except patient_id)\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n\n    # Parse all numeric values\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n\n    # Count missing values before dropping\n    missing_counts = df[numeric_columns].isna().sum()\n    rows_before = len(df)\n\n    # European format: \"12,34\" means 12.34\n        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n        try:\n            return float(value)\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n\n    return np.nan\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n    If value is outside expected range, try converting from alternative units.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Check if already in expected US range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:  # multiply\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        # Check if converted value is in range\n        if us_min <= converted <= us_max:\n            return converted\n\n    # If no conversion helps, keep original value\n    return value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"\n    Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    # Remove any whitespace\n   373→        value = value.strip()\n   374→        if value == '' or value.lower() == 'nan':\n   375→            return np.nan\n   376→\n   377→        # Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n   378→        # European format: \"12,34\" means 12.34\n   379→        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n   380→            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n   381→\n   382→        try:\n   383→            return float(value)\n   384→        except ValueError:\n   385→            return np.nan\n   386→\n   387→    return np.nan\n   388→\n   389→\n   390→def harmonize_value(value, config):\n   391→    \"\"\"\n   392→    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n   393→    If value is outside expected range, try converting from alternative units.\n   394→    \"\"\"\n   395→    if pd.isna(value):\n   396→        return np.nan\n   397→\n   398→    us_min = config['us_min']\n   399→    us_max = config['us_max']\n   400→    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n   401→\n   402→    # Check if already in expected US range\n   403→    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n   404→        return value\n   405→\n   406→    # Try each conversion\n   407→    for factor, direction in conversions:\n   408→        if direction == 'divide':\n   409→            converted = value / factor\n   410→        else:  # multiply\n   411→            converted = value * factor\n   412→\n   413→        # Check if converted value is in range\n   414→        if us_min <= converted <= us_max:\n   415→            return converted\n   416→\n   417→    # Remove any whitespace\n        value = value.strip()\n        if value == '' or value.lower() == 'nan':\n            return np.nan\n\n        # Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n        # European format: \"12,34\" means 12.34\n        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n        try:\n            return float(value)\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n\n    return np.nan\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n    If value is outside expected range, try converting from alternative units.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Check if already in expected US range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:  # multiply\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        # Check if converted value is in range\n        if us_min <= converted <= us_max:\n            return converted\n\n    # If no conversion helps, keep original value\n    return value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"\n    Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    # Read the input data\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    # Get numeric columns (all except patient_id)\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n\n    # Parse all numeric values\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n\n    # Drop rows with any missing values in numeric columns\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - rows_after} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"Remaining rows: {rows_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Harmonizing units...\")\n\n    # Harmonize each column\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        if col in HARMONIZATION_CONFIG:\n            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG[col]\n\n            # Count values out of range before harmonization\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_before = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            # Apply harmonization\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: harmonize_value(x, config))\n\n            # Count values out of range after harmonization\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_after = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            if out_of_range_before > 0:\n                print(f\"  {col}: {out_of_range_before} out of range -> {out_of_range_after} after harmonization\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n\n    # Round all numeric values to 2 decimal places\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: format_value(x, 2))\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Saving harmonized data...\")\n\n    # Save to output file\n    output_path = '/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv'\n\n    # Format the output properly - ensure 2 decimal places in output\n    df_output = df.copy()\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df_output[col] = df_output[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else '')\n\n    df_output.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n    print(f\"Saved harmonized data to: {output_path}\")\n\n    # Print summary\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"Original rows: {original_rows}\")\n    print(f\"Harmonized rows: {len(df)}\")\n    print(f\"Columns: {len(df.columns)}\")\n    print(f\"Rows removed (missing values): {original_rows - len(df)}\")\n\n    # Validate output\n    print(f\"\\nValidation:\")\n    print(f\"  - Checking for scientific notation in output...\")\n    df_check = pd.read_csv(output_path)\n    sci_notation_found = False\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        for val in df_check[col].astype(str):\n            if 'e' in val.lower() or 'E' in val:\n                sci_notation_found = True\n                print(f\"    WARNING: Scientific notation found in {col}: {val}\")\n                break\n    if not sci_notation_found:\n        print(f\"    OK: No scientific notation found\")\n\n    print(f\"  - Checking for comma decimals...\")\n    comma_found = False\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        for val in df_check[col].astype(str):\n            if ',' in val:\n                comma_found = True\n                print(f\"    WARNING: Comma found in {col}: {val}\")\n                break\n    if not comma_found:\n        print(f\"    OK: No comma decimals found\")\n\n    # Check ranges for key analytes\n    print(f\"\\n  - Checking value ranges for key analytes:\")\n    key_cols = ['Serum_Creatinine', 'Hemoglobin', 'Glucose', 'Troponin_I', 'Lactate']\n    for col in key_cols:\n        if col in df.columns:\n            vals = df[col]\n            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG.get(col, {})\n            out_of_range = ((vals < config.get('us_min', 0)) | (vals > config.get('us_max', float('inf')))).sum()\n            print(f\"    {col}: min={vals.min():.2f}, max={vals.max():.2f}, mean={vals.mean():.2f}, out_of_range={out_of_range}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nDone!\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":12,"oldLines":285,"newStart":12,"newLines":345,"lines":[" "," import pandas as pd"," import numpy as np","-import re"," "," # µg/L\n        'conversion_factor': 1, 'direction': 'multiply'\n    },\n}\n\n\ndef parse_numeric_value(value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Parse a numeric value, handling:\n    - Scientific notation (e.g., 1.23e+02)\n    - European decimal format (comma as decimal separator)\n    - Missing/empty values\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):\n        return float(value)\n\n    if isinstance(value, str):\n        # Remove any whitespace\n        value = value.strip()\n        if value == '' or value.lower() == 'nan':\n            return np.nan\n\n        # Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n        # Be careful: only replace comma if it's a decimal separator\n        # European format: \"12,34\" means 12.34\n        # Check if there's already a period\n        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n        try:\n            # Python's float() handles scientific notation automatically\n            return float(value)\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n\n    return np.nan\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n    If value is outside expected range, try converting from alternative units.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    factor = config['conversion_factor']\n    direction = config['direction']\n\n    # Check if already in expected US range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # Try conversion from alternative units\n    if direction == 'divide':\n        converted = value / factor\n    else:  # multiply\n        converted = value * factor\n\n    # Check if converted value is in range\n    if us_min <= converted <= us_max:\n        return converted\n\n    # If conversion doesn't help, keep original value\n    # (might be a true outlier or need manual review)\n    return value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"\n    Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\n    Returns string in format X.XX\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    # Read the input data\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    # Get numeric columns (all except patient_id)\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n\n    # Parse all numeric values\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n\n    # Count missing values before dropping\n    missing_counts = df[numeric_columns].isna().sum()\n    rows_before = len(df)\n\n    # Drop rows with any missing values in numeric columns\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - rows_after} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"Remaining rows: {rows_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Harmonizing units...\")\n\n    # Harmonize each column\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        if col in HARMONIZATION_CONFIG:\n            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG[col]\n\n            # Count values out of range before harmonization\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_before = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            # Apply harmonization\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: harmonize_value(x, config))\n\n            # Count values out of range after harmonization\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_after = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            if out_of_range_before > 0:\n                print(f\"  {col}: {out_of_range_before} values out of range -> {out_of_range_after} after harmonization\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n\n    # Round all numeric values to 2 decimal places\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: format_value(x, 2))\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Saving harmonized data...\")\n\n    # Save to output file\n    output_path = '/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv'\n\n    # Format the output properly - ensure 2 decimal places in output\n    # Create a copy for output formatting\n    df_output = df.copy()\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df_output[col] = df_output[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else '')\n\n    df_output.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n    print(f\"Saved harmonized data to: {output_path}\")\n\n    # Print summary statistics\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"Original rows: {original_rows}\")\n    print(f\"Harmonized rows: {len(df)}\")\n    print(f\"Columns: {len(df.columns)}\")\n    print(f\"Rows removed (missing values): {original_rows - len(df)}\")\n\n    # Validate output\n    print(f\"\\nValidation:\")\n    print(f\"  - Checking for scientific notation in output...\")\n    df_check = pd.read_csv(output_path)\n    sci_notation_found = False\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        for val in df_check[col].astype(str):\n            if 'e' in val.lower() or 'E' in val:\n                sci_notation_found = True\n                print(f\"    WARNING: Scientific notation found in {col}: {val}\")\n    if not sci_notation_found:\n        print(f\"    OK: No scientific notation found\")\n\n    print(f\"  - Checking for comma decimals...\")\n    comma_found = False\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        for val in df_check[col].astype(str):\n            if ',' in val:\n                comma_found = True\n                print(f\"    WARNING: Comma found in {col}: {val}\")\n    if not comma_found:\n        print(f\"    OK: No comma decimals found\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nDone!\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}

Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n        try:\n            return float(value)\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n\n    return np.nan\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n\n    Logic:\n    1. If value is in expected range, keep it\n    2. If value is out of range, try each conversion\n    3. Pick the conversion that brings the value closest to the expected range center\n    4. If no conversion helps (value still out of range after all conversions),\n       use the best conversion if it improves the value\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Target is middle of the expected range\n    range_center = (us_min + us_max) / 2\n\n    # Check if already in expected range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # If no conversions defined, return as-is\n    if not conversions:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion and find the best one\n    best_value = value\n    best_distance = abs(value - range_center)\n    best_in_range = False\n\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        in_range = us_min <= converted <= us_max\n        distance = abs(converted - range_center)\n\n        # Prefer values that are in range\n        # If both in range or both out of range, prefer closer to center\n        if in_range and not best_in_range:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = True\n        elif in_range == best_in_range and distance < best_distance:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = in_range\n\n    return best_value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - rows_after} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"Remaining rows: {rows_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Harmonizing units...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        if col in HARMONIZATION_CONFIG:\n            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG[col]\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_before = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: harmonize_value(x, config))\n\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_after = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            if out_of_range_before > 0:\n                print(f\"  {col}: {out_of_range_before} -> {out_of_range_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: format_value(x, 2))\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Saving harmonized data...\")\n    output_path = '/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv'\n\n    df_output = df.copy()\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df_output[col] = df_output[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else '')\n\n    df_output.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n    print(f\"Saved harmonized data to: {output_path}\")\n\n    # Summary\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"Original rows: {original_rows}\")\n    print(f\"Harmonized rows: {len(df)}\")\n    print(f\"Columns: {len(df.columns)}\")\n    print(f\"Rows removed: {original_rows - Found!' if sci_found else 'OK - None found'}\")\n\n    # Check for commas\n    comma_found = any(',' in str(v) for col in numeric_columns for v in df_check[col].astype(str))\n    print(f\"  Comma decimals: {'WARNING - Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n   378→        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n   379→            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n   380→\n   381→        try:\n   382→            return float(value)\n   383→        except ValueError:\n   384→            return np.nan\n   385→\n   386→    return np.nan\n   387→\n   388→\n   389→def harmonize_value(value, config):\n   390→    \"\"\"\n   391→    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n   392→\n   393→    Logic:\n   394→    1. If value is in expected range, keep it\n   395→    2. If value is out of range, try each conversion\n   396→    3. Pick the conversion that brings the value closest to the expected range center\n   397→    4. If no conversion helps (value still out of range after all conversions),\n   398→       use the best conversion if it improves the value\n   399→    \"\"\"\n   400→    if pd.isna(value):\n   401→        return np.nan\n   402→\n   403→    us_min = config['us_min']\n   404→    us_max = config['us_max']\n   405→    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n   406→\n   407→    # Target is middle of the expected range\n   408→    range_center = (us_min + us_max) / 2\n   409→\n   410→    # Check if already in expected range\n   411→    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n   412→        return value\n   413→\n   414→    # If no conversions defined, return as-is\n   415→    if not conversions:\n   416→        return value\n   417→\n   418→    # Try each conversion and find the best one\n   419→    best_value = value\n   420→    best_distance = abs(value - range_center)\n   421→    best_in_range = False\n   422→\n   423→    for factor, direction in conversions:\n   424→        if direction == 'divide':\n   425→            converted = value / factor\n   426→        else:\n   427→            converted = value * factor\n   428→\n   429→        in_range = us_min <= converted <= us_max\n   430→        distance = abs(converted - range_center)\n   431→\n   432→        # Prefer values that are in range\n   433→        # If both in range or both out of range, prefer closer to center\n   434→        if in_range and not best_in_range:\n   435→            best_value = converted\n   436→            best_distance = distance\n   437→            best_in_range = True\n   438→        elif in_range == best_in_range and distance < best_distance:\n   439→            best_value = converted\n   440→            best_distance = distance\n   441→            best_in_range = in_range\n   442→\n   443→    return best_value\n   444→\n   445→\n   446→def format_value(value, decimals=2):\n   447→    \"\"\"Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\"\"\"\n   448→    if pd.isna(value):\n   449→        return np.nan\n   450→    return round(float(value), decimals)\n   451→\n   452→\n   453→def main():\n   454→    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n   455→\n   456→    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n   457→    original_rows = len(df)\n   458→    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n   459→\n   460→    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n   461→\n   462→    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n   463→    for col in numeric_columns:\n   464→        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n   465→\n   466→    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n   467→    rows_before = len(df)\n   468→    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n   469→    rows_after = len(df)\n   470→    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - rows_after} rows with missing values\")\n   471→    print(f\"Remaining rows: {rows_after}\")\n   472→\n   473→    print(\"\\nStep 3: Harmonizing units...\")\n   474→    for col in numeric_columns:\n   475→        if col in HARMONIZATION_CONFIG:\n   476→            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG[col]\n   477→            values = df[col]\n   478→            out_of_range_before = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n   479→\n   480→            df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: harmonize_value(x, config))\n   481→\n   482→            values = df[col]\n   483→            out_of_range_after = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n   484→\n   485→            if out_of_range_before > 0:\n   486→                print(f\"  {col}: {out_of_range_before} -> {out_of_range_after}\")\n   487→\n   488→    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n   489→    for col in numeric_columns:\n   490→        df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: format_value(x, 2))\n   491→\n   492→    print(\"\\nStep 5: Saving harmonized data...\")\n   493→    output_path = '/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv'\n   494→\n   495→    df_output = df.copy()\n   496→    for col in numeric_columns:\n   497→        df_output[col] = df_output[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else '')\n   498→\n   499→    df_output.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n   500→    print(f\"Saved harmonized data to: {output_path}\")\n   501→\n   502→    # Summary\n   503→    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n   504→    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n   505→    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n   506→    print(f\"Original rows: {original_rows}\")\n   507→    print(f\"Harmonized rows: {len(df)}\")\n   508→    print(f\"Columns: {len(df.columns)}\")\n   509→    print(f\"Rows removed: {original_rows - len(df)}\")\n   510→\n   511→    # Validation\n   512→    print(f\"\\nValidation:\")\n   513→    df_check = pd.read_csv(output_path)\n   514→\n   515→    # Check for scientific notation\n   516→    sci_found = any('e' in str(v).lower() for col in numeric_columns for v in df_check[col].astype(str))\n   517→    print(f\"  Scientific notation: {'WARNING - Found!' if sci_found else 'OK - None found'}\")\n   518→\n   519→    # Check for commas\n   520→    comma_found = any(',' in str(v) for col in numeric_columns for v in df_check[col].astype(str))\n   521→    print(f\"  Comma decimals: {'WARNING - mmHg\n        'conversions': [\n            (7.5006, 'multiply'),  # kPa to mmHg\n        ]\n    },\n    'pO2_Arterial': {\n        'us_min': 30.0, 'us_max': 600.0,  # mmHg\n        'conversions': [\n            (7.5006, 'multiply'),  # kPa to mmHg\n        ]\n    },\n    'Lactate': {\n        'us_min': 0.3, 'us_max': 25.0,  # mmol/L\n        'conversions': [\n            (9.01, 'divide'),  # mg/dL to mmol/L\n        ]\n    },\n\n    # Other Markers\n    'Beta2_Microglobulin': {\n        'us_min': 0.5, 'us_max': 80.0,  # mg/L\n        'conversions': []\n    },\n    'Aluminum': {\n        'us_min': 1.0, 'us_max': 400.0,  # µg/L\n        'conversions': []\n    },\n}\n\n\ndef parse_numeric_value(value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Parse a numeric value, handling:\n    - Scientific notation (e.g., 1.23e+02)\n    - European decimal format (comma as decimal separator)\n    - Missing/empty values\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):\n        return float(value)\n\n    if isinstance(value, str):\n        value = value.strip()\n        if value == '' or value.lower() == 'nan':\n            return np.nan\n\n        # Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n        try:\n            return float(value)\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n\n    return np.nan\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n\n    Logic:\n    1. If value is in expected range, keep it\n    2. If value is out of range, try each conversion\n    3. Pick the conversion that brings the value closest to the expected range center\n    4. If no conversion helps (value still out of range after all conversions),\n       use the best conversion if it improves the value\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Target is middle of the expected range\n    range_center = (us_min + us_max) / 2\n\n    # Check if already in expected range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # If no conversions defined, return as-is\n    if not conversions:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion and find the best one\n    best_value = value\n    best_distance = abs(value - range_center)\n    best_in_range = False\n\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        in_range = us_min <= converted <= us_max\n        distance = abs(converted - range_center)\n\n        # Prefer values that are in range\n        # If both in range or both out of range, prefer closer to center\n        if in_range and not best_in_range:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = True\n        elif in_range == best_in_range and distance < best_distance:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = in_range\n\n    return best_value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - rows_after} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"Remaining rows: {rows_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Harmonizing units...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        if col in HARMONIZATION_CONFIG:\n            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG[col]\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_before = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: harmonize_value(x, config))\n\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_after = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            if out_of_range_before > 0:\n                print(f\"  {col}: {out_of_range_before} -> {out_of_range_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: format_value(x, 2))\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Saving harmonized data...\")\n    output_path = '/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv'\n\n    df_output = df.copy()\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df_output[col] = df_output[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else '')\n\n    df_output.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n    print(f\"Saved harmonized data to: {output_path}\")\n\n    # Summary\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"Original rows: {original_rows}\")\n    print(f\"Harmonized rows: {len(df)}\")\n    print(f\"Columns: {len(df.columns)}\")\n    print(f\"Rows removed: {original_rows - len(df)}\")\n\n    # Validation\n    print(f\"\\nValidation:\")\n    df_check = pd.read_csv(output_path)\n\n    # Check for scientific notation\n    sci_found = any('e' in str(v).lower() for col in numeric_columns for v in df_check[col].astype(str))\n    print(f\"  Scientific notation: {'WARNING - Found!' if sci_found else 'OK - None found'}\")\n\n    # Check for commas\n    comma_found = any(',' in str(v) for col in numeric_columns for v in df_check[col].astype(str))\n    print(f\"  Comma decimals: {'WARNING - Found!' if comma_found else 'OK - None found'}\")\n\n    # Check key ranges\n    print(f\"\\n  Value ranges:\")\n    for col in ['Serum_Creatinine', 'Hemoglobin', 'Glucose', 'Serum_Calcium', 'Lactate']:\n        if col in df.columns:\n            vals = df[col]\n            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG.get(col, {})\n            out_of_range = ((vals < config.get('us_min', 0)) | (vals > config.get('us_max', float('inf')))).sum()\n            print(f\"    {col}: min={vals.min():.2f}, max={vals.max():.2f}, out_of_range={out_of_range}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nDone!\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":15,"oldLines":13,"newStart":15,"newLines":13,"lines":[" "," # Configuration for each lab feature with expected US conventional unit ranges"," # and conversion factors from common alternative units","-# Format: {column_name: [(us_min, us_max, conversion_factor, direction), ...]}","-# Multiple conversion options for complex cases","+# The logic: if value is out of US range, try conversion. Remove any whitespace\n        value = value.strip()\n        if value == '' or value.lower() == 'nan':\n            return np.nan\n\n        # Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n        # European format: \"12,34\" means 12.34\n        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n        try:\n            return float(value)\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n\n    return np.nan\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n    If value is outside expected range, try converting from alternative units.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Check if already in expected US range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:  # multiply\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        # Check if converted value is in range\n        if us_min <= converted <= us_max:\n            return converted\n\n    # If no conversion helps, keep original value\n    return value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"\n    Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    # Apply harmonization\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: harmonize_value(x, config))\n\n            # Count values out of range after harmonization\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_after = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            if out_of_range_before > 0:\n                print(f\"  {col}: {out_of_range_before} out of range -> {out_of_range_after} after harmonization\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n\n    # Round all numeric values to 2 decimal places\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: format_value(x, 2))\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Saving harmonized data...\")\n\n    # Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n        try:\n            return float(value)\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n\n    return np.nan\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n\n    Logic:\n    1. If value is in expected range, keep it\n    2. If value is out of range, try each conversion\n    3. Pick the conversion that brings the value closest to the expected range center\n    4. If no conversion helps (value still out of range after all conversions),\n       use the best conversion if it improves the value\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Target is middle of the expected range\n    range_center = (us_min + us_max) / 2\n\n    # Check if already in expected range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # If no conversions defined, return as-is\n    if not conversions:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion and find the best one\n    best_value = value\n    best_distance = abs(value - range_center)\n    best_in_range = False\n\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        in_range = us_min <= converted <= us_max\n        distance = abs(converted - range_center)\n\n        # Prefer values that are in range\n        # If both in range or both out of range, prefer closer to center\n        if in_range and not best_in_range:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = True\n        elif in_range == best_in_range and distance < best_distance:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = in_range\n\n    return best_value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n        try:\n            return float(value)\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n\n    return np.nan\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n\n    Logic:\n    1. If value is in expected range, keep it\n    2. If value is out of range, try each conversion\n    3. Pick the conversion that brings the value closest to the expected range center\n    4. If no conversion helps (value still out of range after all conversions),\n       use the best conversion if it improves the value\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Target is middle of the expected range\n    range_center = (us_min + us_max) / 2\n\n    # Check if already in expected range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # If no conversions defined, return as-is\n    if not conversions:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion and find the best one\n    best_value = value\n    best_distance = abs(value - range_center)\n    best_in_range = False\n\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        in_range = us_min <= converted <= us_max\n        distance = abs(converted - range_center)\n\n        # Prefer values that are in range\n        # If both in range or both out of range, prefer closer to center\n        if in_range and not best_in_range:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = True\n        elif in_range == best_in_range and distance < best_distance:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = in_range\n\n    return best_value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n        try:\n            return float(value)\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n\n    return np.nan\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n\n    Logic:\n    1. If value is in expected range, keep it\n    2. If value is out of range, try each conversion\n    3. Pick the conversion that brings the value closest to the expected range center\n    4. If no conversion helps (value still out of range after all conversions),\n       use the best conversion if it improves the value\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Target is middle of the expected range\n    range_center = (us_min + us_max) / 2\n\n    # Check if already in expected range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # If no conversions defined, return as-is\n    if not conversions:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion and find the best one\n    best_value = value\n    best_distance = abs(value - range_center)\n    best_in_range = False\n\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        in_range = us_min <= converted <= us_max\n        distance = abs(converted - range_center)\n\n        # Prefer values that are in range\n        # If both in range or both out of range, prefer closer to center\n        if in_range and not best_in_range:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = True\n        elif in_range == best_in_range and distance < best_distance:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = in_range\n\n    return best_value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - rows_after} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"Remaining rows: {rows_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Harmonizing units...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        if col in HARMONIZATION_CONFIG:\n            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG[col]\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_before = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: harmonize_value(x, config))\n\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_after = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            if out_of_range_before > 0:\n                print(f\"  {col}: {out_of_range_before} -> {out_of_range_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: format_value(x, 2))\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Saving harmonized data...\")\n    output_path = '/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv'\n\n    df_output = df.copy()\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df_output[col] = df_output[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else '')\n\n    df_output.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n    print(f\"Saved harmonized data to: {output_path}\")\n\n    # Summary\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"Original rows: {original_rows}\")\n    print(f\"Harmonized rows: {len(df)}\")\n    print(f\"Columns: {len(df.columns)}\")\n    print(f\"Rows removed: {original_rows - len(df)}\")\n\n    # Validation\n    print(f\"\\nValidation:\")\n    df_check = pd.read_csv(output_path)\n\n    # Check for scientific notation\n    sci_found = any('e' in str(v).lower() for col in numeric_columns for v in df_check[col].astype(str))\n    print(f\"  Scientific notation: {'WARNING - Found!' if sci_found else 'OK - None found'}\")\n\n    # Check for commas\n    comma_found = any(',' in str(v) for col in numeric_columns for v in df_check[col].astype(str))\n    print(f\"  Comma decimals: {'WARNING - Found!' if comma_found else 'OK - None found'}\")\n\n    # Check key ranges\n    print(f\"\\n  Value ranges:\")\n    for col in ['Serum_Creatinine', 'Hemoglobin', 'Glucose', 'Serum_Calcium', 'Lactate']:\n        if col in df.columns:\n            vals = df[col]\n            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG.get(col, {})\n            out_of_range = ((vals < config.get('us_min', 0)) | (vals > config.get('us_max', float('inf')))).sum()\n            print(f\"    {col}: min={vals.min():.2f}, max={vals.max():.2f}, out_of_range={out_of_range}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nDone!\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":67,"oldLines":7,"newStart":67,"newLines":7,"lines":["         'conversions': []","     },","     'Magnesium': {","-        'us_min': 1.0, 'us_max': 12.0,  # mg/dL","+        'us_min': 0.5, 'us_max': 12.0,  # mg/dL (expanded for severe hypomagnesemia)","         'conversions': [","             (2.43, 'multiply'),  # mmol/L to mg/dL","         ]"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

If value is in expected range, keep it\n    2. If value is out of range, try each conversion\n    3. Pick the conversion that brings the value closest to the expected range center\n    4. If no conversion helps (value still out of range after all conversions),\n       use the best conversion if it improves the value\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Target is middle of the expected range\n    range_center = (us_min + us_max) / 2\n\n    # Check if already in expected range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # If no conversions defined, return as-is\n    if not conversions:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion and find the best one\n    best_value = value\n    best_distance = abs(value - range_center)\n    best_in_range = False\n\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        in_range = us_min <= converted <= us_max\n        distance = abs(converted - range_center)\n\n        # Prefer values that are in range\n        # If both in range or both out of range, prefer closer to center\n        if in_range and not best_in_range:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = True\n        elif in_range == best_in_range and distance < best_distance:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = in_range\n\n    return best_value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - rows_after} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"Remaining rows: {rows_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Harmonizing units...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        if col in HARMONIZATION_CONFIG:\n            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG[col]\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_before = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: harmonize_value(x, config))\n\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_after = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            if out_of_range_before > 0:\n                print(f\"  {col}: {out_of_range_before} -> {out_of_range_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: format_value(x, 2))\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Saving harmonized data...\")\n    output_path = '/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv'\n\n    df_output = df.copy()\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df_output[col] = df_output[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else '')\n\n    df_output.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n    print(f\"Saved harmonized data to: {output_path}\")\n\n    # Summary\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"Original rows: {original_rows}\")\n    print(f\"Harmonized rows: {len(df)}\")\n    print(f\"Columns: {len(df.columns)}\")\n    print(f\"Rows removed: {original_rows - len(df)}\")\n\n    # Validation\n    print(f\"\\nValidation:\")\n    df_check = pd.read_csv(output_path)\n\n    # Check for scientific notation\n    sci_found = any('e' in str(v).lower() for col in numeric_columns for v in df_check[col].astype(str))\n    print(f\"  Scientific notation: {'WARNING - Found!' if sci_found else 'OK - None found'}\")\n\n    # Check for commas\n    comma_found = any(',' in str(v) for col in numeric_columns for v in df_check[col].astype(str))\n    print(f\"  Comma decimals: {'WARNING - Found!' if comma_found else 'OK - None found'}\")\n\n    # Check key ranges\n    print(f\"\\n  Value ranges:\")\n    for col in ['Serum_Creatinine', 'Hemoglobin', 'Glucose', 'Serum_Calcium', 'Lactate']:\n        if col in df.columns:\n            vals = df[col]\n            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG.get(col, {})\n            out_of_range = ((vals < config.get('us_min', 0)) | (vals > config.get('us_max', float('inf')))).sum()\n            print(f\"    {col}: min={vals.min():.2f}, max={vals.max():.2f}, out_of_range={out_of_range}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nDone!\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":21,"oldLines":7,"newStart":21,"newLines":7,"lines":[" HARMONIZATION_CONFIG = {","     # Kidney Function Markers","     'Serum_Creatinine': {","-        'us_min': 0.3, 'us_max': 25.0,  # mg/dL","+        'us_min': 0.2, 'us_max': 25.0,  # mg/dL (low values possible in muscle wasting)","         'conversions': [","             (88.4, 'divide'),  # µmol/L to mg/dL","         ]"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}
