Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n        try:\n            return float(value)\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n\n    return np.nan\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n\n    Logic:\n    1. If value is in expected range, keep it\n    2. If value is out of range, try each conversion\n    3. Pick the conversion that brings the value closest to the expected range center\n    4. If no conversion helps (value still out of range after all conversions),\n       use the best conversion if it improves the value\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Target is middle of the expected range\n    range_center = (us_min + us_max) / 2\n\n    # Check if already in expected range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # If no conversions defined, return as-is\n    if not conversions:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion and find the best one\n    best_value = value\n    best_distance = abs(value - range_center)\n    best_in_range = False\n\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        in_range = us_min <= converted <= us_max\n        distance = abs(converted - range_center)\n\n        # Prefer values that are in range\n        # If both in range or both out of range, prefer closer to center\n        if in_range and not best_in_range:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = True\n        elif in_range == best_in_range and distance < best_distance:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = in_range\n\n    return best_value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - rows_after} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"Remaining rows: {rows_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Harmonizing units...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        if col in HARMONIZATION_CONFIG:\n            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG[col]\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_before = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: harmonize_value(x, config))\n\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_after = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            if out_of_range_before > 0:\n                print(f\"  {col}: {out_of_range_before} -> {out_of_range_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: format_value(x, 2))\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Saving harmonized data...\")\n    output_path = '/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv'\n\n    df_output = df.copy()\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df_output[col] = df_output[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else '')\n\n    df_output.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n    print(f\"Saved harmonized data to: {output_path}\")\n\n    # Summary\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"Original rows: {original_rows}\")\n    print(f\"Harmonized rows: {len(df)}\")\n    print(f\"Columns: {len(df.columns)}\")\n    print(f\"Rows removed: {original_rows - len(df)}\")\n\n    # Validation\n    print(f\"\\nValidation:\")\n    df_check = pd.read_csv(output_path)\n\n    # Check for scientific notation\n    sci_found = any('e' in str(v).lower() for col in numeric_columns for v in df_check[col].astype(str))\n    print(f\"  Scientific notation: {'WARNING - Found!' if sci_found else 'OK - None found'}\")\n\n    # Check for commas\n    comma_found = any(',' in str(v) for col in numeric_columns for v in df_check[col].astype(str))\n    print(f\"  Comma decimals: {'WARNING - Found!' if comma_found else 'OK - None found'}\")\n\n    # Check key ranges\n    print(f\"\\n  Value ranges:\")\n    for col in ['Serum_Creatinine', 'Hemoglobin', 'Glucose', 'Serum_Calcium', 'Lactate']:\n        if col in df.columns:\n            vals = df[col]\n            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG.get(col, {})\n            out_of_range = ((vals < config.get('us_min', 0)) | (vals > config.get('us_max', float('inf')))).sum()\n            print(f\"    {col}: min={vals.min():.2f}, max={vals.max():.2f}, out_of_range={out_of_range}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nDone!\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":67,"oldLines":7,"newStart":67,"newLines":7,"lines":["         'conversions': []","     },","     'Magnesium': {","-        'us_min': 0.5, 'us_max': 12.0,  # mg/dL (expanded for severe hypomagnesemia)","+        'us_min': 0.4, 'us_max': 12.0,  # mg/dL (expanded for severe hypomagnesemia)","         'conversions': [","             (2.43, 'multiply'),  # mmol/L to mg/dL","         ]"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n        try:\n            return float(value)\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n\n    return np.nan\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n\n    Logic:\n    1. If value is in expected range, keep it\n    2. If value is out of range, try each conversion\n    3. Pick the conversion that brings the value closest to the expected range center\n    4. If no conversion helps (value still out of range after all conversions),\n       use the best conversion if it improves the value\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Target is middle of the expected range\n    range_center = (us_min + us_max) / 2\n\n    # Check if already in expected range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # If no conversions defined, return as-is\n    if not conversions:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion and find the best one\n    best_value = value\n    best_distance = abs(value - range_center)\n    best_in_range = False\n\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        in_range = us_min <= converted <= us_max\n        distance = abs(converted - range_center)\n\n        # Prefer values that are in range\n        # If both in range or both out of range, prefer closer to center\n        if in_range and not best_in_range:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = True\n        elif in_range == best_in_range and distance < best_distance:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = in_range\n\n    return best_value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - rows_after} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"Remaining rows: {rows_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Harmonizing units...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        if col in HARMONIZATION_CONFIG:\n            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG[col]\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_before = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: harmonize_value(x, config))\n\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_after = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            if out_of_range_before > 0:\n                print(f\"  {col}: {out_of_range_before} -> {out_of_range_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: format_value(x, 2))\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Saving harmonized data...\")\n    output_path = '/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv'\n\n    df_output = df.copy()\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df_output[col] = df_output[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else '')\n\n    df_output.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n    print(f\"Saved harmonized data to: {output_path}\")\n\n    # Summary\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"Original rows: {original_rows}\")\n    print(f\"Harmonized rows: {len(df)}\")\n    print(f\"Columns: {len(df.columns)}\")\n    print(f\"Rows removed: {original_rows - len(df)}\")\n\n    # Validation\n    print(f\"\\nValidation:\")\n    df_check = pd.read_csv(output_path)\n\n    # Check for scientific notation\n    sci_found = any('e' in str(v).lower() for col in numeric_columns for v in df_check[col].astype(str))\n    print(f\"  Scientific notation: {'WARNING - Found!' if sci_found else 'OK - None found'}\")\n\n    # Check for commas\n    comma_found = any(',' in str(v) for col in numeric_columns for v in df_check[col].astype(str))\n    print(f\"  Comma decimals: {'WARNING - Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n        try:\n            return float(value)\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n\n    return np.nan\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n\n    Logic:\n    1. If value is in expected range, keep it\n    2. If value is out of range, try each conversion\n    3. Pick the conversion that brings the value closest to the expected range center\n    4. If no conversion helps (value still out of range after all conversions),\n       use the best conversion if it improves the value\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Target is middle of the expected range\n    range_center = (us_min + us_max) / 2\n\n    # Check if already in expected range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # If no conversions defined, return as-is\n    if not conversions:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion and find the best one\n    best_value = value\n    best_distance = abs(value - range_center)\n    best_in_range = False\n\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        in_range = us_min <= converted <= us_max\n        distance = abs(converted - range_center)\n\n        # Prefer values that are in range\n        # If both in range or both out of range, prefer closer to center\n        if in_range and not best_in_range:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = True\n        elif in_range == best_in_range and distance < best_distance:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = in_range\n\n    return best_value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - rows_after} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"Remaining rows: {rows_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Harmonizing units...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        if col in HARMONIZATION_CONFIG:\n            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG[col]\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_before = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: harmonize_value(x, config))\n\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_after = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            if out_of_range_before > 0:\n                print(f\"  {col}: {out_of_range_before} -> {out_of_range_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: format_value(x, 2))\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Saving harmonized data...\")\n    output_path = '/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv'\n\n    df_output = df.copy()\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df_output[col] = df_output[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else '')\n\n    df_output.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n    print(f\"Saved harmonized data to: {output_path}\")\n\n    # Summary\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"Original rows: {original_rows}\")\n    print(f\"Harmonized rows: {len(df)}\")\n    print(f\"Columns: {len(df.columns)}\")\n    print(f\"Rows removed: {original_rows - If value is in expected range, keep it\n    2. If value is out of range, try each conversion\n    3. Pick the conversion that brings the value closest to the expected range center\n    4. If no conversion helps (value still out of range after all conversions),\n       use the best conversion if it improves the value\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Target is middle of the expected range\n    range_center = (us_min + us_max) / 2\n\n    # Check if already in expected range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # If no conversions defined, return as-is\n    if not conversions:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion and find the best one\n    best_value = value\n    best_distance = abs(value - range_center)\n    best_in_range = False\n\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        in_range = us_min <= converted <= us_max\n        distance = abs(converted - range_center)\n\n        # Prefer values that are in range\n        # If both in range or both out of range, prefer closer to center\n        if in_range and not best_in_range:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = True\n        elif in_range == best_in_range and distance < best_distance:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = in_range\n\n    return best_value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n        try:\n            return float(value)\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n\n    return np.nan\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n\n    Logic:\n    1. If value is in expected range, keep it\n    2. If value is out of range, try each conversion\n    3. Pick the conversion that brings the value closest to the expected range center\n    4. If no conversion helps (value still out of range after all conversions),\n       use the best conversion if it improves the value\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Target is middle of the expected range\n    range_center = (us_min + us_max) / 2\n\n    # Check if already in expected range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # If no conversions defined, return as-is\n    if not conversions:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion and find the best one\n    best_value = value\n    best_distance = abs(value - range_center)\n    best_in_range = False\n\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        in_range = us_min <= converted <= us_max\n        distance = abs(converted - range_center)\n\n        # Prefer values that are in range\n        # If both in range or both out of range, prefer closer to center\n        if in_range and not best_in_range:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = True\n        elif in_range == best_in_range and distance < best_distance:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = in_range\n\n    return best_value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - len(df)}\")\n\n    # Validation\n    print(f\"\\nValidation:\")\n    df_check = pd.read_csv(output_path)\n\n    # Check for scientific notation\n    sci_found = any('e' in str(v).lower() for col in numeric_columns for v in df_check[col].astype(str))\n    print(f\"  Scientific notation: {'WARNING - Found!' if sci_found else 'OK - None found'}\")\n\n    # Check for commas\n    comma_found = any(',' in str(v) for col in numeric_columns for v in df_check[col].astype(str))\n    print(f\"  Comma decimals: {'WARNING - Found!' if comma_found else 'OK - None found'}\")\n\n    # Check key ranges\n    print(f\"\\n  Value ranges:\")\n    for col in ['Serum_Creatinine', 'Hemoglobin', 'Glucose', 'Serum_Calcium', 'Lactate']:\n        if col in df.columns:\n            vals = df[col]\n            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG.get(col, {})\n            out_of_range = ((vals < config.get('us_min', 0)) | (vals > config.get('us_max', float('inf')))).sum()\n            print(f\"    {col}: min={vals.min():.2f}, max={vals.max():.2f}, out_of_range={out_of_range}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nDone!\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":132,"oldLines":7,"newStart":132,"newLines":7,"lines":["         'conversions': []","     },","     'Platelet_Count': {","-        'us_min': 20.0, 'us_max': 1500.0,  # K/µL","+        'us_min': 10.0, 'us_max': 1500.0,  # K/µL (low values in thrombocytopenia)","         'conversions': []","     },"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

range_center)\n\n        # Prefer values that are in range\n        # If both in range or both out of range, prefer closer to center\n        if in_range and not best_in_range:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = True\n        elif in_range == best_in_range and distance < best_distance:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = in_range\n\n    return best_value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - rows_after} rows with missing values\")\n    print(f\"Remaining rows: {rows_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 3: Harmonizing units...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        if col in HARMONIZATION_CONFIG:\n            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG[col]\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_before = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: harmonize_value(x, config))\n\n            values = df[col]\n            out_of_range_after = ((values < config['us_min']) | (values > config['us_max'])).sum()\n\n            if out_of_range_before > 0:\n                print(f\"  {col}: {out_of_range_before} -> {out_of_range_after}\")\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 4: Rounding all values to 2 decimal places...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(lambda x: format_value(x, 2))\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 5: Saving harmonized data...\")\n    output_path = '/root/ckd_lab_data_harmonized.csv'\n\n    df_output = df.copy()\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df_output[col] = df_output[col].apply(lambda x: f\"{x:.2f}\" if pd.notna(x) else '')\n\n    df_output.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n    print(f\"Saved harmonized data to: {output_path}\")\n\n    # Summary\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"Original rows: {original_rows}\")\n    print(f\"Harmonized rows: {len(df)}\")\n    print(f\"Columns: {len(df.columns)}\")\n    print(f\"Rows removed: {original_rows - len(df)}\")\n\n    # Validation\n    print(f\"\\nValidation:\")\n    df_check = pd.read_csv(output_path)\n\n    # Check for scientific notation\n    sci_found = any('e' in str(v).lower() for col in numeric_columns for v in df_check[col].astype(str))\n    print(f\"  Scientific notation: {'WARNING - Found!' if sci_found else 'OK - None found'}\")\n\n    # Check for commas\n    comma_found = any(',' in str(v) for col in numeric_columns for v in df_check[col].astype(str))\n    print(f\"  Comma decimals: {'WARNING - Found!' if comma_found else 'OK - None found'}\")\n\n    # Check key ranges\n    print(f\"\\n  Value ranges:\")\n    for col in ['Serum_Creatinine', 'Hemoglobin', 'Glucose', 'Serum_Calcium', 'Lactate']:\n        if col in df.columns:\n            vals = df[col]\n            config = HARMONIZATION_CONFIG.get(col, {})\n            out_of_range = ((vals < config.get('us_min', 0)) | (vals > config.get('us_max', float('inf')))).sum()\n            print(f\"    {col}: min={vals.min():.2f}, max={vals.max():.2f}, out_of_range={out_of_range}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nDone!\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":194,"oldLines":7,"newStart":194,"newLines":7,"lines":["         ]","     },","     'CRP': {","-        'us_min': 0.01, 'us_max': 100.0,  # mg/L","+        'us_min': 0.0, 'us_max': 100.0,  # mg/L","         'conversions': []","     },"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n        try:\n            return float(value)\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n\n    return np.nan\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n\n    Logic:\n    1. If value is in expected range, keep it\n    2. If value is out of range, try each conversion\n    3. Pick the conversion that brings the value closest to the expected range center\n    4. If no conversion helps (value still out of range after all conversions),\n       use the best conversion if it improves the value\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Target is middle of the expected range\n    range_center = (us_min + us_max) / 2\n\n    # Check if already in expected range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # If no conversions defined, return as-is\n    if not conversions:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion and find the best one\n    best_value = value\n    best_distance = abs(value - range_center)\n    best_in_range = False\n\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        in_range = us_min <= converted <= us_max\n        distance = abs(converted - range_center)\n\n        # Prefer values that are in range\n        # If both in range or both out of range, prefer closer to center\n        if in_range and not best_in_range:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = True\n        elif in_range == best_in_range and distance < best_distance:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = in_range\n\n    return best_value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n        try:\n            return float(value)\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n\n    return np.nan\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n\n    Logic:\n    1. If value is in expected range, keep it\n    2. If value is out of range, try each conversion\n    3. Pick the conversion that brings the value closest to the expected range center\n    4. If no conversion helps (value still out of range after all conversions),\n       use the best conversion if it improves the value\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Target is middle of the expected range\n    range_center = (us_min + us_max) / 2\n\n    # Check if already in expected range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # If no conversions defined, return as-is\n    if not conversions:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion and find the best one\n    best_value = value\n    best_distance = abs(value - range_center)\n    best_in_range = False\n\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        in_range = us_min <= converted <= us_max\n        distance = abs(converted - range_center)\n\n        # Prefer values that are in range\n        # If both in range or both out of range, prefer closer to center\n        if in_range and not best_in_range:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = True\n        elif in_range == best_in_range and distance < best_distance:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = in_range\n\n    return best_value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - Replace comma with period for decimal separator\n        if ',' in value and '.' not in value:\n            value = value.replace(',', '.')\n\n        try:\n            return float(value)\n        except ValueError:\n            return np.nan\n\n    return np.nan\n\n\ndef harmonize_value(value, config):\n    \"\"\"\n    Harmonize a single value based on the configuration.\n\n    Logic:\n    1. If value is in expected range, keep it\n    2. If value is out of range, try each conversion\n    3. Pick the conversion that brings the value closest to the expected range center\n    4. If no conversion helps (value still out of range after all conversions),\n       use the best conversion if it improves the value\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Target is middle of the expected range\n    range_center = (us_min + us_max) / 2\n\n    # Check if already in expected range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # If no conversions defined, return as-is\n    if not conversions:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion and find the best one\n    best_value = value\n    best_distance = abs(value - range_center)\n    best_in_range = False\n\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        in_range = us_min <= converted <= us_max\n        distance = abs(converted - range_center)\n\n        # Prefer values that are in range\n        # If both in range or both out of range, prefer closer to center\n        if in_range and not best_in_range:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = True\n        elif in_range == best_in_range and distance < best_distance:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = in_range\n\n    return best_value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before - If value is in expected range, keep it\n    2. If value is out of range, try each conversion\n    3. Pick the conversion that brings the value closest to the expected range center\n    4. If no conversion helps (value still out of range after all conversions),\n       use the best conversion if it improves the value\n    \"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n\n    us_min = config['us_min']\n    us_max = config['us_max']\n    conversions = config.get('conversions', [])\n\n    # Target is middle of the expected range\n    range_center = (us_min + us_max) / 2\n\n    # Check if already in expected range\n    if us_min <= value <= us_max:\n        return value\n\n    # If no conversions defined, return as-is\n    if not conversions:\n        return value\n\n    # Try each conversion and find the best one\n    best_value = value\n    best_distance = abs(value - range_center)\n    best_in_range = False\n\n    for factor, direction in conversions:\n        if direction == 'divide':\n            converted = value / factor\n        else:\n            converted = value * factor\n\n        in_range = us_min <= converted <= us_max\n        distance = abs(converted - range_center)\n\n        # Prefer values that are in range\n        # If both in range or both out of range, prefer closer to center\n        if in_range and not best_in_range:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = True\n        elif in_range == best_in_range and distance < best_distance:\n            best_value = converted\n            best_distance = distance\n            best_in_range = in_range\n\n    return best_value\n\n\ndef format_value(value, decimals=2):\n    \"\"\"Format a numeric value to specified decimal places.\"\"\"\n    if pd.isna(value):\n        return np.nan\n    return round(float(value), decimals)\n\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading CKD lab data...\")\n\n    df = pd.read_csv('/root/environment/data/ckd_lab_data.csv')\n    original_rows = len(df)\n    print(f\"Original data: {original_rows} rows, {len(df.columns)} columns\")\n\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    print(\"\\nStep 1: Converting all numeric values (handling scientific notation and comma decimals)...\")\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        df[col] = df[col].apply(parse_numeric_value)\n\n    print(\"Step 2: Removing rows with missing values...\")\n    rows_before = len(df)\n    df = df.dropna(subset=numeric_columns)\n    rows_after = len(df)\n    print(f\"Removed {rows_before -
